How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Expertise
Categories: Enchantments
[Expertise]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: X
Applied To: [[Fishing Rods]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_EXPERTISE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Expertise I Grants +0.6 Sea Creature Chance and +2☯ Fishing Wisdom when killing Sea Creatures.; 50 kills to tier up!; Applicable on: Fishing Rod Apply Cost: 25 Exp Levels; ▲ Expertise cannot be combined!; COMMON
Expertise is a [[Stacking Enchantments|Stacking]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants [[Fishing Wisdom]] when killing [[Sea Creatures]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]]. It can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]].
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Expertise I|Purchased from [[Elizabeth]] for 4,000 [[Bits]]|
[[Stacking Enchantments]] can't be combined on an [[Anvil]].
### Bazaar
The Expertise [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Expertise|I|
## Ability
Expertise grants [[Fishing Wisdom]] when killing [[Sea Creatures]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]].
|Tier|[[Fishing Wisdom]]|[[Sea Creature Chance]]|[[Sea Creatures]] Killed|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|+2 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+0.6 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|0|
|II|+4 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+1.2 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|50|
|III|+6 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+1.8 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|100|
|IV|+8 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+2.4 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|250|
|V|+10 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+3 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|500|
|VI|+12 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+3.6 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|1,000|
|VII|+14 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+4.2 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|2,500|
|VIII|+16 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+4.8 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|5,500|
|IX|+18 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+5.4 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|10,000|
|X|+20 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|+6 [[Sea Creature Chance]]|15,000|
While the description for Expertise states it grants [[Fishing Wisdom]], it actually grants up to a 1.2x [[Fishing]] XP multiplier.
## Applying
Expertise can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]].
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|25 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- The counter on Expertise increases when a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] fished with a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] with Expertise dies. It does not require the [[Mob]] to be slain with the [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
  - The same applies to the increased [[Fishing]] experience.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Expertise.|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Expertise now levels on the rod that was used to fish Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]), instead of leftmost rod in the hotbar.|

# Fatal Tempo
Categories: Enchantments
[Fatal Tempo]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: V
Applied To: [[Swords]], Gauntlets ([[Gemstone Gauntlet]]), [[Longswords]], [[Bows]], [[Axes]], [[Fishing Rods]]
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XXXVII (37) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_FATAL_TEMPO
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Fatal Tempo I Attacking increases your  Ferocity by 10% per hit, capped at 200% for 3; seconds after your last attack.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Melee Weapon, Weapons Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 37 to apply.; COMMON
Fatal Tempo is an [[Ultimate Enchantments|Ultimate Enchantment]] increasing player's [[Ferocity]] with every hit until 200% boost cap is hit. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Bows]], [[Axes]], [[Fishing Rods]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet|Gauntlets]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXVII (37)]].
## Obtaining
The Fatal Tempo [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained from Paid Chests from [[Kuudra]]:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Fatal Tempo I|0.02% (1 in 4662.0 chests) from Basic Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Fatal Tempo I|0.11% (1 in 922.91 chests) from Hot Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Fatal Tempo I|0.14% (1 in 728.0 chests) from Burning Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Fatal Tempo I|0.17% (1 in 604.76 chests) from Fiery Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Fatal Tempo I|0.2% (1 in 493.58 chests) from Infernal Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Fatal Tempo) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Fatal Tempo V.
### Bazaar
The Fatal Tempo [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Fatal Tempo|I|
|Fatal Tempo|II|
|Fatal Tempo|III|
|Fatal Tempo|IV|
|Fatal Tempo|V|
## Ability
Attacking increases your [[Ferocity]] by 10-50% per hit, capped at 200% for 3 seconds after your last attack.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|10% [[Ferocity]]|
|Ⅱ|20% [[Ferocity]]|
|Ⅲ|30% [[Ferocity]]|
|Ⅳ|40% [[Ferocity]]|
|Ⅴ|50% [[Ferocity]]|
## Applying
The Fatal Tempo [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Swords|Sword]], [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], [[Gemstone Gauntlet|Gauntlet]], [[Axes|Axe]] or [[Bows|Bow]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXVII (37)]] to apply.
Anvil:
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|150 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|200 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|250 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Added Fatal Tempo I–V.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jan 28, 2022|Leaked Fatal Tempo V.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Fatal Tempo I–V.|
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Fixed Fatal Tempo being applied to [[Bows]] by swapping to them from a weapon that have the enchant applied.|
|Jun 22, 2022|Fixed some issues with Fatal Tempo.|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Fixed being able stack Fatal Tempos [[Ferocity]] buff by attacking other players.|
|Sep 29, 2026|Fixed [[Tiger Pet]]’s Apex Predator perk increasing the Fatal Tempo bonus by 30% twice.|
|Sep 29, 2026|Fixed Fatal Tempo activating off its own hits and increasing the player's [[Ferocity]] faster than intended.|

Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_FIRE_ASPECT
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Fire Aspect I Ignites your enemies for 3s, dealing 3% of your damage per second.; Applicable on: Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 15 Exp Levels; COMMON
Fire Aspect is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that ignites enemies, dealing a percentage of the player's damage as [[Damage Calculation|Fire Damage]] per second. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
The Fire Aspect [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Fire Aspect I–II|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Fire Aspect III|Tier IV [[Inferno Demonlord]] drop 1.32% (1 in 75.76 kills)|
### Bazaar
The Fire Aspect [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Fire Aspect|III|
## Ability
Fire Aspect ignites enemies, dealing a percentage of the player's damage per second.
|Tier|Duration|Bonus Damage|
|-|-|-|
|I|3s|3%|
|II|4s|6%|
|III|4s|9%|
## Applying
Fire Aspect can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. Unlocking [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rod II (2)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Fire Aspect.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|15 [[Experience]]|
|II|30 [[Experience]]|
|III|45 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 15, 2026|Fixed Fire Aspect and [[Venomous]] nuking the player during the [[Mage Outlaw]] fight.|
|Oct 22, 2026|Removed Fire Aspect III as a drop from [[Inferno Demonlord]].|
|Oct 22, 2026|Added Fire Aspect III as a drop from [[Millennia-Aged Blaze]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Fire Aspect I–II.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Fire Aspect now deals 100% of the player's [[Damage]] as Fire Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]) over time.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Fire Aspect now displays fire particles instead of physically lighting the target on fire.|
|Feb 2, 2022|Fire Aspect damage is now multiplicative with other damage increments.|
|Feb 2, 2022|Fire Aspect now deals 3% - 6% of the player's [[Damage]] per second.|
|Jun 22, 2022|Fixed Fire Aspect damage being affected by [[Rekindled Ember Armor]] when it's not equipped.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Fire Aspect III as a drop from [[Blaze Slayer]].|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Blaze Rod II (2) ([[Blaze Rod]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Fire Aspect, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Fixed Fire Aspect III not having experience cost to apply.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Fire Aspect now affects [[Mythological]] Mobs.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Fixed Fire Aspect and [[Venomous]] nuking the player during the [[Mage Outlaw]] fight.|

|Ⅵ|+12 [[True Defense]]|
|Ⅶ|+14 [[True Defense]]|

|II|50%|
|III|75%|
|IV|100%|
|V|125%|

# Flame
Categories: Enchantments
[Flame]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: II
Applied To: [[Bows]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_FIRE_ASPECT
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Flame I Arrows ignite your enemies for 3.5s, dealing 3% of your damage per second.; Applicable on: Bow Apply Cost: 25 Exp Levels; COMMON
Flame is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that makes arrows ignites enemies, dealing a percentage of the player's damage as [[Damage Calculation|Fire Damage]] per second. It can be applied to [[Bows]].
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Flame I–II|[[Enchantment Table]]|
## Ability
Flame ignites enemies, dealing a percentage of the player's damage per second.
|Tier|Duration|Bonus Damage|
|-|-|-|
|I|3.5s|3%|
|II|4s|6%|
## Applying
Flame can be applied to [[Bows]]. Unlocking [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rod V (5)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Flame.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|25 [[Experience]]|
|II|50 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Flame I–II.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Reworked Flame to match [[Fire Aspect]].|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Blaze Rod V (5) ([[Blaze Rod]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Flame, instead of enchantment book recipe.|

# Flowstate
Aliases: Flow State, Flow state, Flowstate Enchantment
Categories: Enchantments
[Flowstate]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: III
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XV (15) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ULTIMATE_FLOWSTATE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Flowstate I Consecutive blocks broken grant +1 Mining Speed. Stops after 10s of not mining and caps at 200 blocks.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Mining Tools Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 15 to apply.; COMMON
Flowstate is an Ultimate [[Mining]] [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants increased [[Mining Speed]] per block mined consecutively, up to 200 blocks.
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Flowstate I|Mining Events ([[Dwarven Mines]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Flowstate) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Flowstate III.
### Bazaar
The Flowstate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Flowstate|I|
|Flowstate|II|
|Flowstate|III|
## Ability
Consecutive blocks broken grant [[Mining Speed]] per block broken. The bonus is lost after 10s of not mining and caps at 200 blocks.
|Tier|[[Mining Speed]] per block|max [[Mining Speed]]|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|+1 [[Mining Speed]]|+200 [[Mining Speed]]|
|Ⅱ|+2 [[Mining Speed]]|+400 [[Mining Speed]]|
|Ⅲ|+3 [[Mining Speed]]|+600 [[Mining Speed]]|
## Applying
Flowstate can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XV (15)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|150 [[Experience]]|
## Tips
- The [[Mining Speed]] bonus does not apply if the currently held item is not enchanted with Flowstate. However, this does not reset the timer as swapping back to an item enchanted with it will grant the [[Mining Speed]] bonus.
- The [[Mining Speed]] bonus is accounted for in the [[Heart of the Mountain|Mining Speed Boost]] calculation.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 20, 2024|Added Flowstate enchant.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Added Flowstate enchant.|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Flowstate now unlocked at Enchanting Level 15.|

|Giant Killer VII|25% chance to appear in a Darker Auction ([[Dark Auction]]) while [[Scorpius]] is Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Giant Killer) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Giant Killer V.

|Giant Killer|VII|

Giant Killer increases damage dealt for each percentage of health your target has above you, up to a certain threshold.

|II|+0.2%|10%|

|IV|+0.4%|20%|

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_HARDENED_MANA
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Hardened Vitality I Convert 4% of  Vitality used as  Defense for 10s (max 25 Defense).; Applicable on: Armor Apply Cost: 30 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 22 to apply.; COMMON
Hardened Vitality is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that converts [[Vitality]] used into [[Defense]]. It can be applied to [[Armor]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXII (22)]], and is mutually exclusive with [[Vivacious Vitality]], [[Strong Vitality]], and [[Vampiric Vitality]].
## Obtaining
The Hardened Vitality [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained from Paid Chests from [[Kuudra]]:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Hardened Vitality I|17.7% (1 in 5.65 chests) from Basic Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Hardened Vitality II|16.25% (1 in 6.15 chests) from Hot Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Hardened Vitality III|11.33% (1 in 8.82 chests) from Burning Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Hardened Vitality IV|9.41% (1 in 10.63 chests) from Fiery Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Hardened Vitality V|8.8% (1 in 11.37 chests) from Infernal Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Hardened Vitality I–IX) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Hardened Vitality X.
### Bazaar
The Hardened Vitality [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Hardened Vitality|I|
|Hardened Vitality|II|
|Hardened Vitality|III|
|Hardened Vitality|IV|
|Hardened Vitality|V|
|Hardened Vitality|VI|
|Hardened Vitality|VII|
|Hardened Vitality|VIII|
|Hardened Vitality|IX|
|Hardened Vitality|X|
## Ability
[[Strong Vitality]] converts some percent of [[Vitality]] used into [[Defense]] for 10s, capped at 25 [[Defense]].
|Tier|Conversion percent|[[Vitality]] to reach 25 [[Defense]] cap|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|4%|625 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅱ|8%|312.5 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅲ|12%|208.3 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅳ|16%|156.3 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅴ|20%|125 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅵ|24%|104.2 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅶ|28%|89.3 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅷ|32%|78.1 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅸ|36%|69.4 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅹ|40%|62.5 [[Vitality]]|
## Applying
Hardened Vitality can be applied to [[Armor]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXII (22)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|30 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|60 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|75 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|90 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅵ|180 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅶ|240 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅷ|300 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅸ|360 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅹ|420 [[Experience]]|
Hardened Vitality conflicts with [[Strong Vitality]], [[Vampiric Vitality]], and [[Vivacious Vitality]], meaning that only one of them can be applied to an item at a time.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Added Hardened Mana ([[Hardened Vitality]]).|
|Mar 29, 2022|Made Hardened Mana ([[Hardened Vitality]]) obtainable from [[Kuudra]].|
|Jun 10, 2026|Reworked Hardened Mana ([[Hardened Vitality]]) (Gain 0.1-1% of mana used near you as [[Defense]] for 10 seconds, capped at 400 [[Defense]]) into Hardened Vitality.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Hardened Mana ([[Hardened Vitality]]).|

Impaling can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], [[Bows]], and [[Fishing Rods]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XII (12)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Prismarine Shard|Prismarine Shard I (1)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Impaling.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|10 [[Experience]]|
|II|15 [[Experience]]|
|III|20 [[Experience]]|
|IV|25 [[Experience]]|
|V|30 [[Experience]]|

# Inferno
Categories: Enchantments
[Inferno]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: V
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XXXVII (37) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_INFERNO
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Inferno I Every 10th hit on a mob traps it for 5s and deals 1.25x of that hit's damage over the trap duration.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Melee Weapon, Weapons Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 37 to apply.; COMMON
Inferno is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that traps an [[Mob|enemy]] for a limited time and deals extra damage to it throughout the duration of the trap. The trap damage counts as Magic Damage and is thus affected by mobs with Magic Resistance, especially noticeable against dungeon Minibosses and Bosses.
## Obtaining
The Inferno [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained from Paid Chests from [[Kuudra]]:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Inferno I|0.02% (1 in 4662.0 chests) from Basic Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Inferno I|0.11% (1 in 922.91 chests) from Hot Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Inferno I|0.14% (1 in 728.0 chests) from Burning Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Inferno I|0.17% (1 in 604.76 chests) from Fiery Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Inferno I|0.2% (1 in 493.58 chests) from Infernal Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Inferno) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Inferno V.
### Bazaar
The Inferno [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Inferno|I|
|Inferno|II|
|Inferno|III|
|Inferno|IV|
|Inferno|V|
## Ability
Every 10th hit on a mob traps it for 5s and deals 1 + 0.25x per level of that hit's damage over the trap's duration.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|1.25x|
|Ⅱ|1.5x|
|Ⅲ|1.75x|
|Ⅳ|2x|
|Ⅴ|2.25x|
## Applying
The Inferno [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Swords|Sword]], [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], [[Shredder|Fishing Weapon]], [[Gemstone Gauntlet|Gauntlet]], [[Axes|Axe]] or [[Bows|Bow]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXVII (37)]] to apply.
Anvil:
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|91 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|136 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|179 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|223 [[Experience]]|

Life Steal is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that heals the player for a flat number of [[Health]] upon hitting a mob. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], and is mutually exclusive with [[Drain]] and [[Mana Steal]].
## Obtaining
The Life Steal [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Life Steal I–III|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Life Steal IV|Purchased from [[Fear Mongerer]] for 1,500,000 [[Coins]] and 32 [[Purple Candy]]|
|Life Steal IV|Drop from Grand and above [[Experiments]]|
|Life Steal V|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Life Steal) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Life Steal III.
### Bazaar
The Life Steal [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Life Steal|IV|
|Life Steal|V|
## Ability
Life Steal heals the player for a flat number of [[Health]] upon hitting a mob.
|Tier|[[Health]] per hit|
|-|-|
|I|2.4|
|II|4.8|
|III|7.2|
|IV|9.6|
|V|12|
|VI|14.4|
|VII|16.8|
## Applying
Life Steal can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting V (5)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|20 [[Experience]]|
|II|25 [[Experience]]|
|III|30 [[Experience]]|
|IV|50 [[Experience]]|
|V|200 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- Life Steal, [[Vampirism]] and [[Drain]] receive an additional 1 level of enchantment effect from the [[Reforging|Blood-Soaked]] reforge, which is obtained from the [[Presumed Gallon of Red Paint]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]].
- Life Steal V with the [[Reforging|Blood-Soaked]] reforge heals for 14.4 [[Health]] every time you hit a mob.

# Looting
Aliases: Looting 5, Looting Enchantment
Categories: Enchantments, Article stubs
[Looting]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_LOOTING
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Looting I Increases the chance of a Monster dropping an item by 15%.; Applicable on: Fishing Rod, Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 15 Exp Levels; COMMON
Looting is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases the chances of a mob dropping an item. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], [[Fishing Rods]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
The Looting [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Looting I–III|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Looting IV|Purchased from [[Einary]] for 10 [[North Stars]]|
|Looting V|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Looting) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Looting III.
### Bazaar
The Looting [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Looting|IV|
|Looting|V|
## Ability
Looting increases the chances of a mob dropping an item.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|I|15%|
|II|30%|
|III|45%|
|IV|60%|
|V|75%|
The final drop chance is calculated using the following formula, with chances above 100% representing a chance to drop an additional item.
FinalDropChance=BaseDropChance×(1+(0.15×LootingLevel))
Looting applies whenever a mob dies and the death is attributed to the player holding the weapon with Looting. This means that, for example, if a player using a [[Fire Veil Wand]], switching to a weapon with Looting, and then letting the mob die from their previously cast ability, Looting from the held weapon will apply.
Looting does not apply to [[Loot Sharing|Loot Share]] drops. However, for mobs that have an owner (such as [[Mythological]] mobs and [[Sea Creatures]]), the killer's Looting level applies to the mob's owner.
### Examples
- If a [[Zombie]] has a 100% chance to drop 2 [[Rotten Flesh]], a player with Looting IV would have a 40% chance to obtain a total of 2 [[Rotten Flesh]] and a 60% chance to obtain a total of 4 [[Rotten Flesh]]. They cannot obtain 3 [[Rotten Flesh]] unless the mob has another [[Rotten Flesh]] loot table entry for or some other factor is increasing drops.
- If Player A fishes up a [[Lord Jawbus]], which has a 0.5% base chance to drop a [[Radioactive Vial]] and 0.1% when [[Loot Sharing]], and Player B kills it with Looting III, Player A will have a 0.725% chance to obtain a [[Radioactive Vial]]. Player B and all other players who have received [[Loot Sharing|Loot Share]] will have a 0.1% chance to obtain a [[Radioactive Vial]].
  - Chances may be further buffed by other factors such as [[Magic Find]], which are not covered here.
## Behaviors
This article needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Need interactions related to [[Slayer|Slayers]] [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]].
See more stubs.
- Looting does NOT apply on [[Slayer|Slayers]] nor [[Pets|Pet]] drops.
- Looting effect can compound with external factors such as the [[Mole Pet|Magnetic Nose]] perk from [[Mole Pet]].
- Looting applies BEFORE [[Magic Find]].
  - This leads to many undesirable situations involving a high Looting level as [[Magic Find]] only apply for drops with odds strictly lesser than 5 %.
Example 1:
- Banshees ([[Banshee]]) fished up in the [[Backwater Bayou]] can drop a piece of [[Torn Cloth]](3%). With Looting I, this becomes becomes a 0.03×1.15=3.45% drop chance. [[Magic Find]] still applies. With Looting II, this becomes becomes a 0.03×1.30=3.9% drop chance. [[Magic Find]] still applies. With Looting III, this becomes becomes a 0.03×1.45=4.35% drop chance. [[Magic Find]] still applies. With Looting IV, this becomes becomes a 0.03×1.6=4.8% drop chance. [[Magic Find]] still applies. With Looting V, this becomes becomes a 0.03×1.75=5.25% drop chance. [[Magic Find]] does NOT apply.
- In a situation where multiple players are involved in slaying a [[Mob]] and they all qualify for [[Loot Sharing]] : the player dealing the fatal blow will have its Looting level overwrite that of all other players. However, each player's [[Magic Find]] amount will not be overwritten.
Example 2:
- For example, 4 players - A, B, C and D - start hitting a [[Grim Reaper]]. Their individual Looting levels and effective [[Magic Find]] are: A : Looting IV and 193; B : Looting V and 340; C : forgot to enchant their weapon with Looting, they have 98; D : Looting III and 185; Player A happens to deal the fatal blow. Each player rolls for loot with player A's Looting level specifically and their individual [[Magic Find]] : A : Looting IV and 193; B : Looting IV and 340; C : Looting IV and 98; D : Looting IV and 185
## Applying
Looting can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It does not require an [[Enchanting]] level to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|15 [[Experience]]|
|II|30 [[Experience]]|
|III|45 [[Experience]]|
|IV|100 [[Experience]]|
|V|200 [[Experience]]|

# One For All
Categories: Enchantments, Catacombs Loot
[One For All]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Maximum Level: I
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XXXVIII (38) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_ONE_FOR_ALL
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; One For All I Removes all other enchants but increases your weapon damage by 500%.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Longsword, Sword Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; ▲ One For All cannot be combined!; COMMON
One For All is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that buffs weapon damage massively but removes all other [[Enchantments]].
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|One For All I|Drop from the [[Catacombs]] Floor 7 Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
### Drop Chances
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Obsidian|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.3247% (1 in 308 runs)|0.5614% (1 in 178.1 runs)|290|1|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.6283% (1 in 159.2 runs)|0.986% (1 in 101.4 runs)|290|80|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.1508% (1 in 663 runs)|0.208% (1 in 480.7 runs)|290|1|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.8946% (1 in 111.8 runs)|1.2881% (1 in 77.6 runs)|290|80|
### Bazaar
The One For All [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|One For All|I|
## Ability
Removes all other enchants but increases your damage by 500%.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|500%|
## Applying
The One For All [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Swords|Sword]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXVIII (38)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|45 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]]'s name is referencing the phrase "one for all and all for one" which comes from the book The Three Musketeers, which in turn comes from the Latin phrase: "Unus pro omnibus, omnes pro uno".
  - The name One For All has been further popularized by the show My Hero Academia.
- While this enchantment is generally not worth using for most weapons, due to recent enchant reworks it can actually beat a weapon with [[Chimera|Chimera V]] and full Tier 7 enchantments in DPS (Damage Per Second) if the target is immune to [[Fire Aspect]] and [[Venomous]], especially if the weapon has a multiplicative damage multiplier.
  - A good example of this is the [[Daedalus Blade]] used against [[Mythological]] mobs. However, this is generally not worth using as a sole weapon due to losing out on [[Looting]] – instead, it is only used to lower the mob before killing. It may be worth using to one-tap certain weaker [[Slayer|Slayer Bosses]] though, as their drops are not affected by [[Looting]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 17, 2020|0.9.13|Added One For All I.|
|Feb 2, 2022|Changed One For All from 210% (Multiplicative (Multiplicative buffs are simple multipliers applied at the end, after flat and additive buffs.)) to 500% (Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.)).|
|Nov 17, 2020|0.9.13|One For All I now requires Enchanting XXXVIII (38) ([[Enchanting]]) to apply.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed One For All not having a skill level requirement.|

[Piercing]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: I
Applied To: [[Bows]]
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XVII (17) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_PIERCING
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Piercing I Arrows travel through enemies. The extra targets hit take 25% of the damage.; Applicable on: Bow Apply Cost: 30 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 17 to apply.; COMMON
Piercing is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] makes arrows travel through enemies and hit extra targets. It can be applied to [[Bows]].
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Piercing I|[[Enchantment Table]]|
## Ability
Piercing makes arrows travel through enemies. Extra targets receive a percent of the damage.
|Tier|Damage|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|25%|
## Applying
Piercing can be applied to [[Bows]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XVII (17)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Cactus|Cactus VI (6)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Piercing.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|30 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Sep 30, 2026|Fixed Piercing enchantment not working on Juju Shortbows ([[Juju Shortbow]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Piercing.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Cactus VI (6) ([[Cactus]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Piercing, instead of enchantment book recipe.|

|Power VII|25% chance to appear in a Darker Auction ([[Dark Auction]]) while [[Scorpius]] is Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])|
|Power VII|1 [[Enchanted Book]] (Power VII) from opening Enchanted Book Bundle (Power) ([[Enchanted Book Bundle]]), which is obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].|

|-|-|
|Power|VI|

Power increases bow damage.

|I|+8%|
|II|+16%|
|III|+24%|
|IV|+32%|
|V|+40%|
|VI|+50%|
|VII|+65%|
## Applying
Power can be applied to [[Bows]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting I (I)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Bone|Bone III (3)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Power.

|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Damage is now additive with other enchantments, instead of multiplicative.|

|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Fixed Power not working at all.|

Rainbow is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that allows the player to collect colored wool from Sheep. It can be applied to [[Shears]].

|-|-|
|Rainbow I|[[Enchantment Table]]|

|(same as above)|[[Gift Bag of the Century]]|

Rainbow I causes [[Sheep]] to drop a random [[Wool]] color when sheared.
Rainbow II grants a 50% chance to change the color of a Sheep when shearing it.
Rainbow III grants an 80% chance to change the color of a Sheep when shearing it, and also flips the Sheep upside down.

Rainbow can only be applied to [[Shears]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting VI (6)]] to apply and use.

Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_SHARPNESS
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Sharpness I Increases melee damage dealt by 5%.; Applicable on: Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 10 Exp Levels; COMMON
Sharpness is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases melee damage dealt by the weapon. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
The Sharpness [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Sharpness I|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Sharpness I|Purchased from [[Librarian]] for 30 [[Coins]]|
|Sharpness II–V|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Sharpness VI|Drop from Grand and above [[Experiments]]|
|Sharpness VI|Purchased from [[Tomioka]] for 10,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|Sharpness VII|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
|Sharpness VII|25% chance to appear in a Darker Auction ([[Dark Auction]]) while [[Scorpius]] is Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Sharpness) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Sharpness V.
### Bazaar
The Sharpness [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Sharpness|VI|
|Sharpness|VII|
## Ability
Sharpness increases melee weapon damage. (Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.))
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|I|+5%|
|II|+10%|
|III|+15%|
|IV|+20%|
|V|+30%|
|VI|+40%|
|VII|+50%|
## Applying
Sharpness can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. Unlocking [[Gravel|Gravel IV (4)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Sharpness.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|10 [[Experience]]|
|II|15 [[Experience]]|
|III|20 [[Experience]]|
|IV|25 [[Experience]]|
|V|30 [[Experience]]|
|VI|100 [[Experience]]|
|VII|200 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- [[Aspect of the Jerry, Signature Edition|Aspect of the Jerry Signature Edition]] has an already applied Sharpness X enchantment, but it's damage boost is equal to that of Sharpness VII.
- [[Sword of the Universe]] has an already applied Sharpness L enchantment, which gives 250% [[Damage]] boost.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Sharpness.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Sharpness VI as an item in the [[Dark Auction]].|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Sharpness I crafting recipe to Sharpness IV and made it 8 times more expensive.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Damage is now additive with other enchantments, instead of multiplicative.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed damage being multiplicative instead of additive.|
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Sharpness VI to the [[Tomioka]]'s shop.|
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Sharpness VII as an item in the Darker Auction ([[Dark Auction]]).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Sharpness IV - Sharpness VII can now be dropped from [[Experiments]].|
|Feb 2, 2022|Changed scaling from 5/10/15/20/25/30/35% to 5/10/15/20/30/45/65% bonus damage.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Gravel IV (4) ([[Gravel]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Sharpness, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Removed Sharpness IV - Sharpness V from [[Experiments]].|
|Aug 14, 2025|Sharpness can now be used with other mob-specific enchantments.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Changed scaling from 5/10/15/20/30/45/65% to 5/10/15/20/30/40/50% bonus damage.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Sharpness VI is no longer obtainable from [[Dark Auction]].|

|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Silk Touch I|[[Enchantment Table]]|
## Ability
Items applied with the Silk Touch allow the player to collect normally unobtainable block drops.
## Applying
Silk Touch can be applied to [[Tools]]. It does not require an [[Enchanting]] level to apply. Unlocking [[String|String V (5)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Silk Touch.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|10 [[Experience]]|
Silk Touch conflicts with [[Fortune]] and [[Smelting Touch]], meaning that only one of them can be applied to an item at a time.

[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Silk Touch.|
|Jun 17, 2019|Fixed using Silk Touch on Red ([[Red Mushroom Block]]) and Brown Mushroom Blocks ([[Brown Mushroom Block]]) resulting in a block named "null".|

|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|Halved the sell price of Silk Touch.|
|Jul 11, 2019|Fixed an issue with items stacking when using Silk Touch.|
|Jul 21, 2019|Fixed some double tall slabs dropping null blocks when broken with Silk Touch.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Mining blocks with Silk Touch now gives [[Farming]] experience.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Breaking a double slab block will now drop 2 slabs instead of only 1 when using Silk Touch.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|String V (5) ([[String]]) now grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Silk Touch, instead of enchantment book recipe.|

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Smelting Touch I Automatically smelts broken blocks into their smelted form.; Applicable on: Mining Tools Apply Cost: 5 Exp Levels; COMMON
Smelting Touch is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that automatically smelts broken blocks so they drop their smelted form. It can be applied to [[Tools]].
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Smelting Touch I|[[Enchantment Table]]|
## Ability
Smelting Touch automatically smelts items dropped when breaking block.
## Applying
Smelting Touch can be applied to [[Tools]]. Unlocking [[Coal|Coal II (2)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Smelting Touch.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|5 [[Experience]]|
Smelting Touch conflicts with [[Silk Touch]], meaning that only one of them can be applied to an item at a time.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Smelting Touch.|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|Changed Smelting Touch to work on more block drops.|
|Aug 19, 2020|Smelting Touch now works on a wider variety of tools.|
|Jun 22, 2022|Fixed Smelting Touch disabling [[Farming Fortune]].|
|Feb 14, 2023|0.18|[[Farming Fortune]] will now also work with a combination of Smelting Touch.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; Stated to be fixed in Jun 22, 2022 patch, did it break again?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Fixed Smelting Touch not counting drops towards Crop Milestones ([[The Garden]])|
|Sep 12, 2023|0.19.4|Using Smelting Touch on [[Netherrack]] now provides Netherrack collection.|
|Nov 14, 2023|0.19.7|Fixed a collection exploit with Smelting Touch.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Fixed Smelting Touch counting towards [[Netherrack]] collection on Private Islands ([[Private Island]]) when the player placed the block.|
|Oct 16, 2023|Fixed Smelting Touch granting too much progress in [[Collections]] and Jacob's Farming Contests ([[Jacob's Farming Contest]]).|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Coal II (2) ([[Coal]]) now grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Smelting Touch, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Sep 19, 2024|Fixed Smelting Touch not working on items going into [[Stash]].|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_SMITE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Smite I Increases damage dealt to  Skeletal,  Undead and  Wither mobs by 5%.; Applicable on: Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 10 Exp Levels; COMMON
Smite is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases damage dealt towards [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]], [[Wither]], and [[Skeletal]] mobs. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
The Smite [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Smite I|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Smite I|Purchased from [[Librarian]] for 20 [[Coins]]|
|Smite II–V|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Smite VI|Tier III [[Revenant Horror]] drop 0.4% (1 in 250.7 kills) Tier IV [[Revenant Horror]] drop 0.4% (1 in 248.74 kills) Tier V [[Revenant Horror]] drop 0.34% (1 in 293.94 kills)|
|Smite VII; [[Severed Hand]] Endcap Upgrade ([[Endcaps]])|Tier V [[Revenant Horror]] drop 0.05% (1 in 2,099.57 kills)|
|(same as above)|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Smite) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Smite V.
### Bazaar
The Smite [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Smite|VI|
|[[Severed Hand]]|[[Severed Hand]]|
## Ability
Smite increases damage against [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]], [[Wither]], and [[Skeletal]] mobs.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|I|+5%|
|II|+10%|
|III|+15%|
|IV|+20%|
|V|+30%|
|VI|+40%|
|VII|+50%|
## Applying
Smite can be applied to [[Swords]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting I (I)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Rotten Flesh|Rotten Flesh III (3)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Smite.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|10 [[Experience]]|
|II|15 [[Experience]]|
|III|20 [[Experience]]|
|IV|25 [[Experience]]|
|V|30 [[Experience]]|
|VI|100 [[Experience]]|
|VII|200 [[Experience]]|

## Ability
Your weapon gains 2-10x the Mob [[Damage]] of the latest monster killed and adds it as [[Strength]] on your next critical hit.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|2x|
|Ⅱ|4x|
|Ⅲ|6x|
|Ⅳ|8x|
|Ⅴ|10x|
## Applying
The Soul Eater [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Swords|Sword]], [[Bows|Bow]] or [[Longswords|Longsword]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXVI (36)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|91 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|136 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|179 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|223 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]]'s name could be a reference to the Dementor's Kiss, performed by Dementors from the Harry Potter franchise, in which they suck the soul out of someone.
  - It could also be a reference to the TV manga series Soul Eater.
- The damage from the [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] is affected by player's [[Crit Damage]], so the final damage would be increased by 2-10 * monster's [[Damage]] * (1 + [[Crit Damage]] / 100) on critical hits.

# Strong Vitality
Aliases: Strong Mana
Categories: Enchantments
[Strong Vitality]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_STRONG_MANA
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Strong Vitality I Convert 2% of  Vitality used as  Strength for 10s (max 15 Strength).; Applicable on: Armor Apply Cost: 30 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 22 to apply.; COMMON
Strong Vitality is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that converts [[Vitality]] used into [[Strength]]. It can be applied to [[Armor]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXII (22)]], and is mutually exclusive with [[Hardened Vitality]], [[Vivacious Vitality]], and [[Vampiric Vitality]].
## Obtaining
The Strong Vitality [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained from Paid Chests from [[Kuudra]]:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Strong Vitality I|17.7% (1 in 5.65 chests) from Basic Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Strong Vitality II|16.25% (1 in 6.15 chests) from Hot Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Strong Vitality III|11.33% (1 in 8.82 chests) from Burning Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Strong Vitality IV|9.41% (1 in 10.63 chests) from Fiery Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Strong Vitality V|8.8% (1 in 11.37 chests) from Infernal Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Strong Vitality I–IX) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Strong Vitality X.
### Bazaar
The Strong Vitality [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Strong Vitality|I|
|Strong Vitality|II|
|Strong Vitality|III|
|Strong Vitality|IV|
|Strong Vitality|V|
|Strong Vitality|VI|
|Strong Vitality|VII|
|Strong Vitality|VIII|
|Strong Vitality|IX|
|Strong Vitality|X|
## Ability
Strong Vitality converts some percent of [[Vitality]] used into [[Strength]] for 10s, capped at 15 [[Strength]].
|Tier|Conversion percent|[[Vitality]] to reach 15 [[Strength]] cap|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|2%|750 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅱ|4%|375 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅲ|6%|250 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅳ|8%|187.5 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅴ|10%|150 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅵ|12%|125 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅶ|14%|107.1 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅷ|16%|93.8 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅸ|18%|83.3 [[Vitality]]|
|Ⅹ|20%|75 [[Vitality]]|
## Applying
Strong Vitality can be applied to [[Armor]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXII (22)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|30 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|60 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|75 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|90 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅵ|180 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅶ|240 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅷ|300 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅸ|360 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅹ|420 [[Experience]]|
Strong Vitality conflicts with [[Hardened Vitality]], [[Vampiric Vitality]], and [[Vivacious Vitality]], meaning that only one of them can be applied to an item at a time.

# Sunset
Aliases: Moonlit, Sunset Enchantment
Categories: Enchantments, Mob Drops

[Enchantment Requirements]

Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XXVII (27) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_SUNSET
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Sunset I During the day, +1  Overbloom.; During the night, -1% Visitor Cooldown.; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Armor Apply Cost: 50 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 27 to apply.; COMMON
  Not to be confused with [[Chocolate Rabbits|Sunset]], a Beach Faction Rabbit.
Sunset is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants [[Overbloom]] during the day and reduces [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] cooldown while breaking crops during the night. It can be applied to [[Armor]].
## Obtaining
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Sunset I|[[Lunar Moth]] drop (35%* ([[Pests]]))|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Sunset) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Sunset V.
### Bazaar
The Sunset [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Sunset|I|
|Sunset|II|
|Sunset|III|
|Sunset|IV|
|Sunset|V|
## Ability
Sunset grants increased [[Overbloom]] during the day and reduces Visitor cooldown while breaking crops during the night.
|Tier|Bonus [[Overbloom]]|Visitor Cooldown Reduction|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|+1 [[Overbloom]]|-1%|
|Ⅱ|+2 [[Overbloom]]|-2%|
|Ⅲ|+3 [[Overbloom]]|-3%|
|Ⅳ|+4 [[Overbloom]]|-4%|
|Ⅴ|+5 [[Overbloom]]|-5%|
## Applying
The Sunset [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to [[Armor]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXVII (27)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|150 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|200 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|250 [[Experience]]|
## Tips
Using a [[Day Saver]] or [[Night Saver]] can allow the player to set the time on their [[The Garden|Garden]], allowing them to gain the bonus from day or night for as long as the time is selected. Due to Sunset only giving [[Overbloom]] during the day, it is imposible to farm [[Moonflower|Moonflowers]] with the [[Overbloom]] bonus.

Thorns is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants a 50% chance to rebound 3-12% [[Damage]] dealt back at the attacker. It can be applied to [[Armor]], and conflicts with [[Reflection]].
## Obtaining
The Thorns [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Thorns I–III|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Thorns IV; [[Prickly Creeper]] Endcap Upgrade ([[Endcaps]])|Requirement: None, Ingredients: 7x [[Enchanted Cactus]] 16x [[Cactus Flower]] 64x [[Enchanted Gunpowder]], [[Crafting]] recipe: Recipe: [[Cactus Flower]] x16 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] ×7 + [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] x64 → [[Prickly Creeper]]|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Thorns) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Thorns III.
### Bazaar
The Thorns [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] cannot be traded through the [[Bazaar]], but the [[Prickly Creeper]] can.
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|[[Prickly Creeper]]|[[Prickly Creeper]]|
## Ability
Items applied with Thorns grants a 50% chance to rebound 3-12% [[Damage]] dealt back at the attacker, scaling with its level.
|Tier|[[Damage]] percent|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|3%|
|Ⅱ|6%|
|Ⅲ|9%|
|Ⅳ|12%|
## Applying
Thorns can be applied to [[Armor]]. Unlocking [[Cactus|Cactus VII (7)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on it.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|15 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|30 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|
Thorns conflicts with [[Reflection]].
## Tips
- It is recommended for [[Reflection]] and Thorns to be removed for the [[Livid]] boss fight, as it can make the Livid Clones teleport erratically.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Thorns.|
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Nerfed from +10% [[Damage]] to +3% [[Damage]] per level.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added an Achievement ([[Achievements]]) for creating a Thorns III book.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed the recipe to Thorns II, and made it twice as expensive.|
|Aug 15, 2019|Fixed Thorns to actually work.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed Magma Armor ([[Armor of Magma]]) not counting kills with Thorns.|
|Oct 17, 2019|Thorns no longer applies invulnerability ticks on Mobs ([[Mob]]).|
|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Removed the [[Damage]] cap on Thorns.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Thorns not removing [[Reflection]] on chestplates.|
|Sep 12, 2023|0.19.4|Thorns is no longer prevented from activating by [[Soul Eater]].|

## Ability
Turbo-Crop grants [[Crop Fortune]].
Turbo-Crop IV and above require the player achieved certain [[Medals]] in the corresponding [[Crops (disambiguation)|Crop]]'s [[Jacob's Farming Contest]]. If the medal requirement isn't met, the [[Crop Fortune]] bonus is capped at the highest medal requirement achieved. For example if a player has Turbo-Crop VII but only a [[Jacob's Farming Contest|BRONZE]] medal, they will receive +20 [[Crop Fortune]].
|Tier|[[Crop Fortune]]|Medal ([[Medals]]) Requirement|

# Vampiric Vitality
Aliases: Mana Vampire

[Enchantment Requirements]

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Vampiric Vitality I Heal for 0.55 per  Vitality used.; Applicable on: Armor Apply Cost: 30 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 22 to apply.; COMMON
Vampiric Vitality is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that heals [[Health]] when [[Vitality]] is used. It can be applied to [[Armor]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXII (22)]], and is mutually exclusive with [[Hardened Vitality]], [[Strong Vitality]], and [[Vivacious Vitality]].
## Obtaining
The Vampiric Vitality [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained from Paid Chests from [[Kuudra]]:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|

|Vampiric Vitality II|16.25% (1 in 6.15 chests) from Hot Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Vampiric Vitality III|11.33% (1 in 8.82 chests) from Burning Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Vampiric Vitality IV|9.41% (1 in 10.63 chests) from Fiery Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
|Vampiric Vitality V|8.8% (1 in 11.37 chests) from Infernal Kuudra ([[Kuudra]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Vampiric Vitality I–IX) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Vampiric Vitality X.
### Bazaar
The Vampiric Vitality [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].

|-|-|

|Vampiric Vitality|VII|
|Vampiric Vitality|VIII|
|Vampiric Vitality|IX|
|Vampiric Vitality|X|
## Ability
Vampiric Vitality heals for some amount of [[Health]] per [[Vitality]] used.
|Tier|Healing|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|0.55 [[Health]]|
|Ⅱ|0.6 [[Health]]|
|Ⅲ|0.65 [[Health]]|
|Ⅳ|0.7 [[Health]]|
|Ⅴ|0.75 [[Health]]|
|Ⅵ|0.8 [[Health]]|
|Ⅶ|0.85 [[Health]]|
|Ⅷ|0.90 [[Health]]|
|Ⅸ|0.95 [[Health]]|
|Ⅹ|1 [[Health]]|
## Applying
Vampiric Vitality can be applied to [[Armor]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXII (22)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|30 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|60 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|75 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|90 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅵ|180 [[Experience]]|

Vampiric Vitality conflicts with [[Hardened Vitality]], [[Strong Vitality]], and [[Vivacious Vitality]], meaning that only one of them can be applied to an item at a time.

# Vampirism
Categories: Enchantments, Temp/Using Skill Aliases
[Vampirism]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_VAMPIRISM
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Vampirism I Heal 5 every time you kill a mob.; (0.5s Cooldown).; Applicable on: Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 20 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 15 to apply.; COMMON
Vampirism is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that heals the player for a flat number of their missing [[Health]] upon killing a mob. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
The Vampirism [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Vampirism I–V|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Vampirism VI|Opening [[Trick or Treat Chest]] Opening [[Party Chest]] Iron bracket (1500 points) or higher in [[Spooky Festival]]|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Vampirism) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Vampirism V.
### Bazaar
The Vampirism [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Vampirism|VI|
## Ability
Vampirism heals the player for a flat number of their missing [[Health]] upon killing a mob. [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] has a 0.5s cooldown.
|Tier|[[Health]] upon kill|
|-|-|
|I|5|
|II|10|
|III|15|
|IV|20|
|V|25|
|VI|30|
## Applying
Vampirism can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XV (15)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Ghast Tear|Ghast Tear IV (4)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Vampirism.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|20 [[Experience]]|
|II|25 [[Experience]]|
|III|30 [[Experience]]|
|IV|40 [[Experience]]|
|V|50 [[Experience]]|
|VI|200 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- Vampirism, [[Life Steal]] and [[Drain]] receive an additional 1 level of enchantment effect (+5 [[Health]] to healing in Vampirism case) from the [[Reforging|Blood-Soaked]] reforge, which is obtained from the [[Presumed Gallon of Red Paint]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]].
- Healing from Vampirism is 10x less effective in the [[Catacombs]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 10, 2026|Changed Vampirism to heal flat numbers of [[Health]] upon killing a mob, instead of 1%/2%/3%/4%/5%/6% of the player's missing [[Health]].|
|Jun 10, 2026|Added a 0.5s cooldown.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Vampirism I–V.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Vampirism I crafting recipe to Vampirism IV and made it 8 times more expensive.|
|Oct 24, 2019|0.7.3|Added Vampirism VI as a reward from [[Spooky Festival]].|
|Oct 30, 2020|0.9.9|Vampirism VI can now drop from Trick or Treat Chests ([[Trick or Treat Chest]]).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Vampirism I–VI can now drop from [[Experiments]].|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Ghast Tear IV (4) ([[Ghast Tear]]) now grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Vampirism, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Vampirism VI can now drop from Party Chests ([[Party Chest]]).|
|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Removed Vampirism I–VI from [[Experiments]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed Vampirism to heal flat numbers of [[Health]] upon killing a mob, instead of 1%/2%/3%/4%/5%/6% of the player's missing [[Health]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Added a 0.5s cooldown.|

Venomous is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that reduces the target's walk speed and deals a percentage of the player's [[Damage]] per second, stacking up to 40 hits and lasting for 5s. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining

|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|

|(same as above)|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Venomous) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Venomous III.
### Bazaar
The Venomous [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Venomous|VI|
|[[Fateful Stinger]]|[[Fateful Stinger]]|

Venomous reduces the target's walk speed and deals a percentage of the player's [[Damage]] per second, stacking up to 40 hits and lasting for 5s.
|Tier|Walk Speed Reduction|Damage %|
|-|-|-|
|I|2%|+0.2%|
|II|4%|+0.4%|
|III|6%|+0.6%|
|IV|8%|+0.8%|
|V|12%|+1.2%|
|VI|15%|+1.6%|
|VII|20%|+2%|
## Applying
Venomous can be applied to [[Swords]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XVII (17)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Spider Eye|Spider Eye VI (6)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Venomous.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|20 [[Experience]]|
|II|25 [[Experience]]|
|III|30 [[Experience]]|
|IV|40 [[Experience]]|
|V|50 [[Experience]]|
|VI|200 [[Experience]]|
|VII|0 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- The walk speed reduction from Venomous is reduced by -80% while in the [[Catacombs]].

|Nov 4, 2025|0.23.6|Changed bonus damage scaling to +0.2/0.4/0.6/0.8/1.2/1.6%.|
|Nov 4, 2025|0.23.6|Changed walk speed reduction scaling from 5/10/15/20/25/30% to 2/4/6/8/12/15%.|
|Nov 4, 2025|0.23.6|Added [[Fateful Stinger]], which is used to apply Venomous VII on an item.|

# Ultimate Enchantments
Aliases: Enchantments (ultimate), Ultimate Enchant, Ultimate Enchantment, Ultimate Enchants, Ultimate enchant, Ultimate enchants
Categories: Enchants
[Ultimate Enchantments]
Ultimate Enchantments are more powerful [[Enchantments]]. Only one Ultimate Enchantment can be added to an item.
## Usage
Ultimate Enchantments can be applied to items in an Anvil. An item can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment at a time. If attempting to apply another Ultimate Enchantment to an item already with an Ultimate Enchantment, the previous Ultimate Enchantment will be overwritten.

## XP Levels
The levels needed for applying Ultimate Enchantments are listed in the following table:
|Ultimate Enchantment level|Cost to apply to an item|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|50 levels|
|Ⅱ|100 levels|
|Ⅲ|150 levels|
|Ⅳ|200 levels|
|Ⅴ|250 levels|
## Bazaar
Ultimate Enchantment Books can be bought and sold on the [[Bazaar]].
- [[Enchanted Book/Bazaar (Ultimate)|Tab 1]]
## Trivia
- Several Ultimate Enchantments reference other parts of popular culture, such as TV shows and other video games.
- All armor Ultimate Enchantments are stack-able on separate armor pieces.
  - This makes it possible to get to 100% fewer coins lost on death, i.e. no coins lost at all.
- Sometimes [[Last Stand]] and [[Wisdom (Enchantment)|Wisdom]] won't work when they reach the enchantment's threshold.
- The achievement [[Achievements|Tough Choice]] can be obtained from combining 2 Ultimate Enchantment books.
- Previously, [[Habanero Tactics]] used to increase damage dealt by Axes, Scythes, Daggers and Katanas. The effect only applied to items that had the corresponding [[Sword Types|Sword Type]], rather than the type in its name. For example it did not work on tool [[Axes]]
- [[One For All]] is the only ultimate enchantments to only have one tier.
- [[Bobbin' Time]], [[Habanero Tactics]] and [[The One]] are the only ultimate enchantments without tier 1.

|Nov 18, 2020|Fixed Soul Eater granting too much damage.|
|Nov 29, 2020|Fixed [[Ultimate Wise]] not working with [[Flower of Truth]].|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Reduced cost of applying Ultimate Enchantments to items to 45, 91, 136, 179, 223 (from 50, 100, 150, 200, 250).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Ultimate Enchantments now require a certain Enchanting skill level to apply and use (except [[One For All]]).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Nerfed [[Rend]] to only work with 5 arrows at a time.|
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Description of [[Legion]] now reads "Increases most of your player stats by..." instead of "Increases all your player stats except Health by..." This is because [[Legion]] does not increase the new stats [[Mining Speed]], [[Mining Fortune]], [[Foraging Fortune]] and [[Farming Fortune]].|
|Apr 30, 2020|Fixed [[Ultimate Wise]] not showing the correctly reduced mana cost for Wither Impact.|

|Aug 13, 2021|[[Rend]] only counts the shots on the mob which is being Rended|

|Feb 2, 2022|Changed [[One For All]] to become additive 500% (from multiplicative 210%)|
|Feb 2, 2022|Doubled the drop chance of Chimera I ([[Chimera]]) books from Minos Inquisitors ([[Minos Inquisitor]]).|

|May 5, 2022|Added Flash I–V ([[Flash]]).|
|Aug 5, 2022|Added Habanero Tactics IV–V ([[Habanero Tactics]]).|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Changed Bank ([[Bank (Enchantment)]]) to additionally drop 0.5 [[Coins]] per level.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Changed [[Combo]] to increase damage by 1% per level for each kill up to 2 kills per level within 2s per level, instead of increasing [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]] by a fixed amount for increasing durations.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Fixed a bug with [[Rend]] that caused defense to apply twice. Now deals [[True Damage]].|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Changed [[Swarm]] to increase damage by 2% per level for each nearby enemy, up to a maximum of 10 enemies, instead of 1.25% per level for each nearby enemy up to a maximum of 16 enemies.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Ultimate Enchantment books are now sold on the [[Bazaar]].|

|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added The One IV–V ([[The One]]).|

The Abyssal Miner Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Pickaxe Cooldown]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Abyssal Miner Shard can be obtained from killing an [[Abyssal Miner]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
An Abyssal Miner Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Abyssal Miner Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Pickaxe Cooldown]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Pickaxe Cooldown ([[Attributes]]); Reduces the cooldown of your Pickaxe Ability by +1%–10%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Abyssal Miner Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

The Adaptive Belt is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Belts|Belt]] obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Adaptive Belt is a reward from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|Obsidian|500,000 [[Coins]]|6.4621% (1 in 15.5 runs)|9.1891% (1 in 10.9 runs)|240|3|
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|500,000 [[Coins]]|7.1683% (1 in 14 runs)|9.7466% (1 in 10.3 runs)|240|3|
## Usage
Belt:
Adaptive Belt [[Health]]: +10 [[Defense]]: +5 [[Strength]]: +5
[Effects]
[Grants additional bonuses based on your selected dungeon class! Berserk: +10 Strength Healer: +10 Health, +5 Mending Mage: +25 Intelligence Tank: +5 Health, +10 Defense Archer: +2 Crit Chance, +5 Crit Damage]
Belt:
[[Adaptive Belt]] [[Health]]: +15 [[Defense]]: +10 [[Strength]]: +10
[Effects]
[Grants additional bonuses based on your selected dungeon class! Berserk: +10 Strength Healer: +10 Health, +5 Mending Mage: +25 Intelligence Tank: +5 Health, +10 Defense Archer: +2 Crit Chance, +5 Crit Damage]
The Adaptive Belt, similar to [[Adaptive Armor]] but separate from its full set, grants bonus stats depending on the user's selected [[Classes|class]] in the [[Catacombs]].
- [[Berserk|Berserks]] gain +10 [[Strength]].
- [[Healer|Healers]] gain +10 [[Health]] and +5 [[Mending]].
- [[Mage|Mages]] gain +25 [[Intelligence]].
- [[Tank|Tanks]] gain +5 [[Health]] and +10 [[Defense]].
- [[Archer|Archers]] gain +2 [[Crit Chance]] and +5 [[Crit Damage]].
## Upgrading
An Adaptive Belt can be upgraded into Adaptive Belt using 8 [[Scarf Fragment|Scarf Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +5 [[Strength]]
- +5 [[Health]]
- +5 [[Defense]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Adaptive Belt]] 1x Adaptive Belt 8x [[Scarf Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Scarf Fragment]] ×8 + Adaptive Belt → [[Adaptive Belt]]|

Tooltip: Gear Score: 220 (237); Damage: +170 (+187); Gemstones: [☤]; As a weapon created by Scarf, it automatically adapts to its user inside Dungeons.; Berserk: +10 Damage, +75 Strength, +10 Speed Healer: +10 Mending & tethered players and players within 10 blocks are healed for 33%; of your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism healing.; Mage: +100 Intelligence, +10 Damage & melee attacks restore 25% additional mana.; Tank: +100 Defense, +5 Vitality, +5 True Defense Archer: Your melee attacks cause enemies to take 10% more damage from your arrows for 5 seconds.; Right-click to use your class ability!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor II Completion.; EPIC DUNGEON SWORD
An Adaptive Blade is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Dungeon Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
An Adaptive Blade can be claimed once as a reward from [[Scarf|Scarf IV (4)]], which is obtained after defeating [[Scarf]] 150 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Adaptive Blade is a reward from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor II]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|Emerald|1,200,000 [[Coins]]|0.8929% (1 in 112 runs)|0.9009% (1 in 111 runs)|250|1|
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|Obsidian|1,500,000 [[Coins]]|3.2258% (1 in 31 runs)|5.8321% (1 in 17.1 runs)|250|2|
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]]) (Master Mode)|Emerald|1,200,000 [[Coins]]|0.9433% (1 in 106 runs)|1.3077% (1 in 76.5 runs)|250|1|
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|1,500,000 [[Coins]]|3.921% (1 in 25.5 runs)|6.0688% (1 in 16.5 runs)|250|2|
## Usage
Sword:
Adaptive Blade [[Damage]]: +170
[Effects]
[As a weapon created by Scarf, it automatically; adapts to its user inside Dungeons.; Berserk: +10 Damage, +75 Strength, +10 Speed; Healer: +10 Mending & tethered players and; players with 10 blocks are healed for 33%; of your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism healing.; Mage: +100 Intelligence, +10 Damage &; melee attacks restore 25% additional mana.; Tank: +100 Defense, ++5 Vitality, +5 True Defense; Archer: Your melee attacks cause enemies to; take 10% more damage from your arrows for; 5 seconds.]
While inside the [[Catacombs]], the Adaptive Blade gains increased stats depending on the wielder's selected [[Classes|Class]].
- [[Berserk|Berserks]] gain +10 [[Damage]], +75 [[Strength]], and +10 [[Speed]].
- [[Healer|Healers]] gain +10 [[Mending]]: additionally, tethered players and players within 10 blocks are healed for 33% of healing gained from [[Life Steal]], [[Drain]], and [[Vampirism]].
- [[Mage|Mages]] gain +100 [[Intelligence]] and +10 [[Damage]]: additionally, melee attacks restore 25% additional mana.
- [[Tank|Tanks]] gain +100 [[Defense]], +5 [[Vitality]], and +5 [[True Defense]].
- [[Archer|Archers]] gain the ability for melee attacks cause enemies to take 10% more damage from arrows for 5 seconds.
Right-click to use your class ability!
### Upgrading
An Adaptive Blade can be upgraded into Adaptive Blade using 8 [[Scarf Fragment|Scarf Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +20 [[Damage]]
- ☤ Defensive [[Gemstone Slot]] becomes a ⚔ Combat [[Gemstone Slot]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Adaptive Blade]] 1x Adaptive Blade 8x [[Scarf Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Scarf Fragment]] ×8 + Adaptive Blade → [[Adaptive Blade]]|
### Fragged
Sword:
[[Adaptive Blade]] [[Damage]]: +190
[Effects]
[As a weapon created by Scarf, it automatically; adapts to its user inside Dungeons.; Berserk: +10 Damage, +75 Strength, +10 Speed; Healer: +10 Mending & tethered players and; players with 10 blocks are healed for 33%; of your Life Steal, Drain, and Vampirism healing.; Mage: +100 Intelligence, +10 Damage &; melee attacks restore 25% additional mana.; Tank: +100 Defense, ++5 Vitality, +5 True Defense; Archer: Your melee attacks cause enemies to; take 10% more damage from your arrows for; 5 seconds.]
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|15|15|
|✪✪|25|40|
|✪✪✪|40|80|
|✪✪✪✪|60; 10,000 [[Coins]]|140; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|100; 25,000 [[Coins]]|240; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- While the Adaptive Blade is generally outclassed by items such as a [[Hyper Cleaver]], it is especially not recommended for [[Archer|Archers]] as the Adaptive Blade is a sword, and Archers primarily use bows for damage. Additionally, Archers deal reduced melee damage, making this item almost obsolete.
The only exception to the above rule is a strategy used in m7 to increase ultimate damage via swapping to the weapon.
## Trivia
- The Adaptive Blade and [[Adaptive Armor]] are references to League of Legends' Armor Adaptive Armor and Helm Adaptive Helm.

## Obtaining
### Fusion
An Aero Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|EPIC]] and Combat Category
- Second: [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher

## Usage

- Wind Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Strength]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Albino Hunter Bat Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Albino Hunter Bat Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on October 24, 2026. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 950 purchases.
## Usage
The Albino Hunter Bat Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Bat Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Alligator Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Echo of Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Alligator Shard can be obtained from killing an [[Alligator]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Huntraps
An Alligator Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Backwater Bayou]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
An Alligator Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Crocodile Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Combat Category

# Amber Material
Aliases: Ambered
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Forged items
Put a bunch of Amber together and it'll make more Amber
— Throin the Forger
[Amber Material]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Put a bunch of Amber together and it'll make more Amber - Throin the Forger.
[Reforge/Power Stone]

Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXX (30) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 76,800x (1,200 stacks (76,800 items total)) Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 5x [[Glacite Jewel]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Gold Ingot]] 51,200x [[Diamond]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: AMBER_MATERIAL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Ambered reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Put a bunch of Amber together and it'll make more Amber - Throin the Forger.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed; Ambered Bonus Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
The Amber Material is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Ambered]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]].
## Obtaining
The Amber Material can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VI (6)]]. It requires 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]], 1 [[Golden Plate]], and 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Amber Material]] 1 Amber Material
  - 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]] 960 (15 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Amber Gemstone]] 76,800 (1,200 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Amber Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Amber Material can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Ambered]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Speed]], which scales with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Ambered]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Ambered Bonus, which makes [[Golden Goblin|Golden]] and [[Diamond Goblin|Diamond Goblins]] 40% more common when mining.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+25 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+35 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+50 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+75 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+100 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+150 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+200 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Ambered Bonus; Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.|Ambered Bonus; Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.|Ambered Bonus; Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.|Ambered Bonus; Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.|Ambered Bonus; Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.|Ambered Bonus; Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.|Ambered Bonus; Find Goblins from mining 40% more often.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The quote of the lore of this item may be a reference to Thorin Oakenshield, a character from The Hobbit by J. R. R. Tolkien. In The Hobbit, Thorin is a dwarf, which is relevant as this item is created in [[The Forge]], which is located in the [[Dwarven Mines]], the home of the Dwarves.
  - However, both names are slightly different (Throin and Thorin). Additionally, Thorin was never a forger (he was a king that was exiled, as mentioned in the story).

## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
An Arachne Crystal can be purchased from [[Shaggy]] for 2 [[Arachne Fragment|Arachne Fragments]], 16 [[Enchanted Spider Eye|Enchanted Spider Eyes]] and 16 [[Enchanted String]] after completing his quest of killing [[Arachne]].

- It takes at least 8 Lv300 [[Arachne]] fights to acquire all materials needed to make an Arachne Crystal, if sourced exclusively from the fight.

## Obtaining

## Usage
Item:
Archaic Spade
[Effects]
[Use to reveal and dig up Griffin Burrows in the Hub (Hub Island) while Diana Diana's Mythological Ritual is active.; Maximum Burrow Chain Length: 6 Ability: Echo RIGHT CLICK; Show the way to the next or nearby Griffin burrow.; Cooldown: 2s]
The Archaic Spade's Echo ability points the player towards the nearest [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] when used on the [[Hub Island]]. It can be also used to dig out burrows by left clicking them.
The available loot from treasure burrows is as follows, depending on the tier of the spade. It does not depend on the rarity of the player's [[Griffin Pet]].
