|[[Coins]] x5000|5,000 [[Coins]]|100|16.89% (1 in 5.92 treasures)|3.52% (1 in 28.42 burrows)|3.82% (1 in 26.15 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x10k|10,000 [[Coins]]|40|6.76% (1 in 14.8 treasures)|1.41% (1 in 71.04 burrows)|1.53% (1 in 65.37 burrows)|
|[[Mythos Fragment]]|[[Mythos Fragment]]|40|6.76% (1 in 14.8 treasures)|1.41% (1 in 71.04 burrows)|1.53% (1 in 65.37 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x25k|25,000 [[Coins]]|20|3.38% (1 in 29.6 treasures)|0.70% (1 in 142.08 burrows)|0.76% (1 in 130.74 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x50k|50,000 [[Coins]]|10|1.69% (1 in 59.2 treasures)|0.35% (1 in 284.16 burrows)|0.38% (1 in 261.49 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x100k|100,000 [[Coins]]|5|0.84% (1 in 118.4 treasures)|0.18% (1 in 568.32 burrows)|0.19% (1 in 522.97 burrows)|
|[[Coins]] x250k|250,000 [[Coins]]|2|0.34% (1 in 296 treasures)|0.07% (1 in 1,420.8 burrows)|0.07% (1 in 1,353.14 burrows)|
|[[Myth the Fish]]|[[Myth the Fish]]||0.01% (1 in 10,000 treasures)|0.0021% (1 in 48,000 burrows)|0.0022% (1 in 45,714.29 burrows)|
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Deific Spade]]|None|1x [[Hilt of Revelations]] 1x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] [[Archaic Spade]] 1x Archaic Spade 16x [[Mythos Fragment]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 128x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] x64 ×2 + [[Braided Griffin Feather]] + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x64 ×2 + Archaic Spade + [[Mythos Fragment]] x8 ×2 + [[Hilt of Revelations]] → [[Deific Spade]]|
## Trivia
- The maximum length of a [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] chain achievable with the Archaic Spade is 6, or 8 with the [[Reforging|Erudite]] reforge when [[Daedalus' Notes]] is applied to it.
- When using the Archaic Spade's ability, there is a 1% chance to play a random game sound instead of the usual sound, likely as an Easter egg.

# Archfiend Dice
Categories: Epic items, Items, Slayer, Blaze Slayer
[Archfiend Dice]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Drop Chance: 7Extraordinary ([[Drop Chance]])
Obtained: [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier III+
[Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[High Class Archfiend Dice]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ARCHFIEND_DICE
Tooltip: Ability: Try Your Luck RIGHT CLICK Costs 666.7k coins to roll between 1-6.; Gain between -120 and +120 for 24h.; Overwritten by new rolls!; If you roll a 6, earn 15M coins but lose this dice.; Heavy in the hand. Could someone have tampered with it?; EPIC
Archfiend Dice are an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
Archfiend Dice are an [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]] drop obtained from the [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier III+ with [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer IV (4)]]. They have a 1.08% chance to drop from Tier III Bosses, and a 1.02% chance to drop from Tier IV Bosses.
## Usage
Item:
Archfiend Dice
[Effects]
[Ability: Try Your Luck RIGHT CLICK; Costs 666.7k coins to roll between 1-6.; Gain between -120❤ and +120❤ for 24h.; Overwritten by new rolls!; If you roll a 6, earn 15M coins but lose this dice.; Heavy in the hand. Could someone have tampered with it?]
To use an Archfiend Dice, it costs 666,666 [[Coins]]. It will roll, and land on a number between 1 and 7. The player can gain between +120 [[Health]] and -120 [[Health]]. This effect lasts for 24 hours.
The dice first attempts to roll for a 7 (0.015% (1 in 6,666 rolls), can be buffed 2x–3x by [[Vincent]]). If this fails, it attempts to roll for a 6 (4.17% (1 in 24 rolls)). If this fails too, it rolls for a number between 1 and 5 with equal chances.
If the dice lands on a 6, the player gains 15,000,000 [[Coins]], but loses the dice. If the dice lands on a 7, the player gains an [[Archfiend Dye]], but loses the dice.
## 1x Archfiend Dye
|Number|Chance|Result|Loses Dice|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-120 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|2|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-80 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|3|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-40 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|4|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|+40 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|5|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|+80 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|6|4.17% (1 in 24 rolls)|+120 [[Health]]; 15,000,000 [[Coins]]|✔ Yes|
|7|0.015% (1 in 6,666 rolls)|[[Archfiend Dye]]|✔ Yes|
## 2x Archfiend Dye
|Number|Chance|Result|Loses Dice|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-120 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|2|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-80 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|3|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-40 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|4|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|+40 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|5|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|+80 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|6|4.17% (1 in 24.01 rolls)|+120 [[Health]]; 15,000,000 [[Coins]]|✔ Yes|
|7|0.030% (1 in 3,333 rolls)|[[Archfiend Dye]]|✔ Yes|
## 3x Archfiend Dye
|Number|Chance|Result|Loses Dice|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-120 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|2|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-80 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|3|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|-40 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|4|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|+40 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|5|19.16% (1 in 5.22 rolls)|+80 [[Health]]|✕ No|
|6|4.16% (1 in 24.01 rolls)|+120 [[Health]]; 15,000,000 [[Coins]]|✔ Yes|
|7|0.045% (1 in 2,222 rolls)|[[Archfiend Dye]]|✔ Yes|
## Notes
- The dice can be used even without [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer IV (4)]].
## Trivia
- The poll former admin 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon made on the 26th of February 2022 on gambling most likely helped inspire this item.
- Its description implies that the dice is rigged not in favor of the player.
- The dice have negative expected value.
- [[Sinful Dice]], [[Archfiend Dice]] Archfiend Dice and [[High Class Archfiend Dice]] are one of few items in the game whose texture varies between items. Each has 6 texture variants, one for each side of the die.
  - Unlike all other items, [[Lucky Dice]] only has one. It is also the only Dice that got a new texture in the SkyBlock Resource Pack.
    - Oddly enough, the old sprite of [[Lucky Dice]], which had 5 pips on top is different than the sprite of 5 pips on top of any other Dice item.
    - Despite there being only 6 different sprites of Dice items, there are actually 48 possible variants of shown sides of a standard dice from an angle where 3 sides are shown.
  - When this item has been dropped on the ground and picked back up by a player, or when switched away from and back to in the hotbar, it will change its texture randomly to one of its six texture variants.

The Architect's First Draft is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Dungeon Items|Dungeon Item]] that can be used to reset a failed [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|Dungeon Puzzle]] in the room the player is in. It is consumed on use.

The Architect's First Draft can be obtained from secret chests on any [[Catacombs]] floor.

Armor of the Pack, also known as Pack Armor, is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] set unlocked at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VI (6)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
The Armor of the Pack requires 512 (8 stacks) [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] and 10 [[Golden Tooth]] to craft.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Helmet of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]])|Wolf Slayer VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|3x [[Golden Tooth]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] x32 ×4 → [[Armor of the Pack]]|
|Chestplate of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]])|Wolf Slayer VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|4x [[Golden Tooth]] 128x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] x32 ×4 + [[Golden Tooth]] ×4 → [[Armor of the Pack]]|
|Leggings of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]])|Wolf Slayer VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Golden Tooth]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] x32 ×6 + [[Golden Tooth]] → [[Armor of the Pack]]|
|Boots of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]])|Wolf Slayer VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|2x [[Golden Tooth]] 64x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] x32 ×2 → [[Armor of the Pack]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Helmet of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]]); [[Health]]: +70 [[Defense]]: +65 +225 additional [[Defense]] and 20 [[True Defense]] against [[Animal]] mobs.|Chestplate of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]]); [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +95 +225 additional [[Defense]] and 20 [[True Defense]] against [[Animal]] mobs.|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Leggings of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]]); [[Health]]: +80 [[Defense]]: +75 +225 additional [[Defense]] and 20 [[True Defense]] against [[Animal]] mobs.|Boots of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]]); [[Health]]: +50 [[Defense]]: +45 +225 additional [[Defense]] and 20 [[True Defense]] against [[Animal]] mobs.|
[Total]
[Armor of the Pack Health: +300 Defense: +280]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Armor of the Pack; Gain +35 Strength and +80 Defense for each Armor of the Pack wearers within 30 blocks. Max of 3 players]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Leggings of the Coven]]|Vampire Slayer III (3) ([[Vampire Slayer]])|1x [[Femurgrowth Leggings]] 1x Leggings of the Pack ([[Armor of the Pack]]) 1x [[Bloodbadge]] 24x [[Living Metal Heart]]|Recipe: [[Femurgrowth Leggings]] + [[Bloodbadge]] + [[Armor of the Pack]] + [[Living Metal Heart]] x6 ×4 → [[Leggings of the Coven]]|
## Trivia
- The armor set has never been popular due to its impracticality in an actual combat situation. Even if you work ahead of time to assemble a class of players that all own the armor, the set bonus still isn't as useful as it seems. The bonuses it provides are even more redundant as the [[Mastiff Armor]], unlocked earlier at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer IV (4)]] is much more practical for taking on the slayer boss, especially when used in tandem with the [[Pooch Sword]].
  - If the leggings are transferred to [[Rift Dimension]], its item lore will change to "Imagine an armor piece so bad that it need to travel to another dimension to become relevant". An obvious joke based on its unpopularity.
- The Helmet of the Pack is one of 6 armor pieces which have the hex color code of ⁠ #FFFFFF. The others are [[Heavy Armor|Heavy Helmet]], [[Heavy Armor|Heavy Boots]], [[Kelly's T-Shirt]], [[Leggings of the Coven]] (white variant) and [[Snow Suit Armor]].
- The Chestplate of the Pack shares the Color ⁠ #FF0000 with [[Mushroom Armor]].

Raw Materials to upgrade: 163,840x [[Obsidian]] 4,096x [[Null Sphere]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ASPECT_OF_THE_VOID
Tooltip: Damage: +120; Strength: +100; Gemstones: []; Ability: Instant Transmission RIGHT CLICK Teleport 8 blocks ahead of you and gain +50  Speed for 3 seconds.; Mana Cost: 45; This item can be reforged!; EPIC SWORD
The Aspect of the Void is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] and an upgrade to the [[Aspect of the End]]. It is unlocked as a recipe at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VI (6)]]. However, it does not require [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VI (6)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VI (6) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Aspect of the End]] 32x [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] x8 ×4 + [[Aspect of the End]] → Aspect of the Void|
## Usage
Sword:
Aspect of the Void [[Damage]]: +120 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Instant Transmission RIGHT CLICK; Teleport 8 blocks ahead of you and gain +50 Speed for 3 seconds.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 45]
The Aspect of the Void isn't good for melee damage at the stage of the game players will be in when obtaining, but its abilities have great usage throughout the game.
When the ability is used, it arrests its user's momentum and teleports them 8 blocks in the direction they are looking. This teleport cannot pass through walls, including iron bars, but can pass through 1-block holes in iron bar walls. After teleporting, its user gains 50 [[Speed]] for 3s. The potency of this speed boost does not stack with multiple uses, but the duration does. The ability has no cooldown and costs 45 [[Mana]] (50 [[Mana]] for the [[Aspect of the End]]). Like other mobility items, the ability cannot be used in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss room. The bonuses provided from a full set of [[Strong Dragon Armor]] that affect the Aspect of the End also apply to the Aspect of the Void.
It can be upgraded with [[Transmission Tuner|Transmission Tuners]] to increase its range by 1 additional block. It can be upgraded up to 4 times for a maximum of 12 total blocks. If an [[Aspect of the End]] has been upgraded with the maximum number of Tuners, the resulting Aspect of the Void cannot receive any more.
Like the Aspect of the End, it can be reforged to Warped with a [[Warped Stone]], granting it +165 [[Damage]] and +165 [[Strength]]. With this, it can be stronger than a fully maxed 50m Midas Sword when used outside [[Dungeons]], provided it has the same upgrades. However, since it cannot be dungeonized anymore, it is not the best option for Dungeons.
### Etherwarp Conduit
It can be combined with an [[Etherwarp Conduit]] and an [[Etherwarp Merger]]. This grants it the [[Etherwarp Conduit]]'s long-range teleport ability when right clicking while sneaking.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer VII (7) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Etherwarp Merger]] 1x [[Etherwarp Conduit]] [[Aspect of the Void]] 1x Aspect of the Void|Recipe: Aspect of the Void + [[Etherwarp Merger]] + [[Etherwarp Conduit]] → Aspect of the Void|
## Trivia
- Unlike the [[Aspect of the End]], when using the ability in 1.8.9, the user does not slow down to sneaking speed due to the blocking animation. It also can be held down to continuously teleport, making it more efficient than an Aspect of the End. However, if a player plays in a version which is later than 1.8.9, the Aspect of the End and Aspect of the Void only had the 5 Mana difference.
- Comparing both the [[Aspect of the End]] and Aspect of the Void with only the [[Ultimate Wise|Ultimate Wise V]] enchants on each, the [[Mana]] cost would change to 25 [[Mana]] for the [[Aspect of the End]], and 23 [[Mana]] for the Aspect of the Void (2 [[Mana]] cost difference).
- Using the sword's ability just before hitting the ground will cancel any fall damage.
- The item ability will always teleport the player to an integer Y coordinate, and 0.5 + an integer X and Z coordinates, which means that every time you use the ability, you will always end up in the middle of a block.
- The item ability Instant Transmission is likely a reference to the technique used in Dragon Ball with the same name.
- Despite the recipe being locked behind [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL VI (6)]], this item can be used before the player unlocks the recipe, meaning there's no requirements for using this item.
- When reforged to Warped, it and its prior form, the [[Aspect of the End]], were the only non-accessory Dungeon items which could not be upgraded with stars, which was a feature that was later removed.
  - If it was reforged away from Warped, it would retain its status as a Dungeon item.
- When this item first released, it used to be able to teleport in The Catacombs - Floor VII boss room using Ether Transmission when combined with a [[Etherwarp Conduit]].
  - This was patched very soon after the bug was found.
- Prior to the 0.19.10 update, it was better to reforge the Aspect of the Void to [[Reforging|Heroic]] for more [[Intelligence]] once the player had a better weapon to use for damage, but now the [[Reforging|Warped]] reforge gives higher [[Intelligence]], as well as [[Damage]] and [[Strength]].

|Jul 10, 2026|Changed Aspect of the Void texture.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

Item ID: ATOMINIZER
Tooltip: Right-Click on a Exe, Wai or Zee Atom to harvest their orbs.; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; EPIC
The Atominizer is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is used to obtain [[X]], [[Y]], and [[Z]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Atominizer can be bought from the [[Mage Emissary]] for 100,000 [[Coins]] and 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Enchanted Mycelium]].
## Usage
Right-Clicking an [[Exe]], [[Wai]], or [[Zee]] with an Atominizer in hand will harvest one of the atom's orbs. This will give the player an [[X]], [[Y]], or [[Z]], for using the item on an [[Exe]], [[Wai]], or [[Zee]] respectively. One of the atom's experience orbs orbiting it will disappear, and half of its [[Health]] will be removed. An Atominizer can be used on an atom twice before killing it.

The Awakened Eye Backpack Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Backpack Skins|Backpack Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Awakened Eye Backpack Skin could be purchased from the Spring 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.
## Usage
The Awakened Eye Backpack Skin can be applied to a [[Backpack|Backpacks]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

The Azure Sea Slug Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Azure Sea Slug Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 2, 2023. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 2,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Azure Sea Slug Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Slug Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Baby Blue Ender Dragon Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on July 29, 2023. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 10,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Baby Blue Ender Dragon Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Ender Dragon Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.
